To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [On-Demand Service Zone Design for Energy-Constrained Spatial Queueing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26765)
*Peng Lin,Cheng Hua,Wei Qi,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出一种能量约束的超立方体空间排队模型，将电池状态动态嵌入经典超立方体框架，并开发了分支-定价-评估框架求解联合选址-分区问题。研究表明：明确能量建模可减少虚假服务承诺；需求低时宜采用聚合分区，需求高时宜采用紧凑分区；分区设计对盈利的影响超过电池改进，且更大电池在稀疏需求下可能降低舰队就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动服务车辆的电池限制和充电需求显著影响调度可行性和空间部署决策，传统模型未充分考虑能量约束，导致服务承诺不切实际。

Method: 采用半马尔可夫表示的**能量约束超立方体空间排队模型**，将电池状态动态嵌入经典超立方体框架；构建**混合整数非线性规划**的联合选址-分区问题；提出**分支-定价-评估框架**处理列系数无闭式表达的分区重构问题，用上界代理指导定价，迭代精确评估更新活动列系数。

Result: 1) 显式能量建模显著减少虚假服务承诺；2) 存在负载依赖的分区反转：低需求时聚合更优，高需求时紧凑分区更优；3) 分区设计对盈利的驱动作用大于电池改进；4) 更大电池在稀疏需求下可能延迟补给并降低舰队就绪度。

Conclusion: 能量可行性不仅是电池容量扩展问题，更是塑造服务区配置的设计维度；管理者应先优化服务区设计，再投资电池升级。

Abstract: Electric service vehicles (ESVs), such as mobile chargers and drone-based service units, are becoming an important operational resource for on-demand service systems. Unlike conventional spatial servers, ESV operations are shaped by battery limits and recharging needs, which affect dispatch feasibility and spatial deployment decisions. We develop an energy-constrained hypercube spatial queueing model that embeds battery-state dynamics into the classical hypercube framework and uses a semi-Markov representation to estimate steady-state performance. We then formulate a joint location--zoning problem for station placement and service zone design. The resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear program admits a set partitioning reformulation whose column coefficients are not available in closed form. We therefore develop a Branch-Price-and-Evaluation framework for set partitioning problems with externally computable column coefficients: upper-bounding surrogates guide pricing, and iterative exact evaluation updates the coefficients of active columns. Computational results show that explicit energy modeling significantly reduces false service promises and yields more credible planning decisions. They also reveal a load-dependent reversal in zoning: pooling is preferable under light demand, whereas tighter zoning becomes more profitable as demand increases. Over the tested range, profitability is driven more by zoning than by battery improvement, suggesting that managers should get service zone design right before investing in battery upgrades; this caution is reinforced by the counterintuitive finding that larger batteries may delay replenishment and reduce fleet readiness under sparse demand. These findings show that energy feasibility is not merely a matter of battery-capacity expansion, but a design dimension that shapes service-zone configuration.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [The Kernel's Write: Application Read-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26138)
*Hui Sub Shim,Katherine Mohr,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出AROM机制，重新设计硬件/软件接口，将LtRAM管理从DIMM控制器转移到操作系统，通过只读页面和写时复制技术，在读取密集型负载下匹配纯DRAM性能，同时获得LtRAM的密度和成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM成本下降趋缓，成为数据中心增长的瓶颈；LtRAM虽具有密度和成本优势，但其非对称读写延迟、有限耐久性和粗粒度写入等缺陷导致当前采用闪存式翻译层的方案引入了显著延迟，需要重新设计接口来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出AROM（Application Read-Only Memory）设计：LtRAM页面对应用只读，仅由OS在页面迁移时写入；利用写时复制（CoW）机制，应用写入LtRAM时触发缺页错误，将页面迁移回DRAM后再执行写操作；将LtRAM管理从DIMM控制器转移到操作系统层面。

Result: 简化了DIMM硬件设计；在读取密集型负载下能匹配纯DRAM的性能；同时保留LtRAM的密度和成本优势。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计硬件/软件接口，将LtRAM管理权交给操作系统，可以避开传统翻译层带来的延迟开销，在保持成本密度优势的同时实现接近DRAM的读取性能。

Abstract: Alongside power, DRAM has become a major limiting factor in datacenter growth. As DRAM's cost-per-bit has plateaued over the past decade, a class of emerging memory technologies, called Long-term RAM (LtRAM), offers a path to denser and cheaper main memory.
  However, LtRAM has three main drawbacks: asymmetric read/write latencies, limited endurance, and coarse write granularity. In an attempt to isolate software from these drawbacks, LtRAM technologies such as Intel Optane copy an approach from flash devices and introduce a translation layer that manages wear-leveling, address remapping, and read/write caching. Prior experimental studies have found these operations add significantly to LtRAM latency.
  Rather than making LtRAM look like DRAM, we propose redesigning the hardware/software interface to offload more responsibility to the operating system. This design hinges on one central property, Application Read-Only Memory (AROM): LtRAM pages are read-only to applications and written only by the OS during page migrations. AROM is enforced by leveraging copy-on-write (CoW): application writes to LtRAM trigger a fault that migrates the page back to DRAM before the store is applied. This invariant allows us to shift LtRAM management from the on-DIMM controller to the operating system, drastically simplifying the DIMM's hardware. With this approach, we aim to match the performance of pure DRAM on read-mostly workloads while delivering LtRAM's density and cost advantages.

</details>


### [145] [Mitigating High-Frequency Geometric Noise in Non-Parametric 1-Bit Sparse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26137)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于确定性、非参数化双流形执行框架的神经形态计算方法，将128维整数向量映射到1024维超完备空间，通过硬激活阈值产生超稀疏1比特二进制种群码。针对线性重建中出现的高频几何噪声伪影，采用硬件级低通滤波器即可消除，实现近乎零的重构误差，为边缘AI提供了一种免乘法器的稳定替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习硬件依赖高功耗的浮点运算，能效较低。神经形态计算需要替代性的数据编码范式，以降低计算能耗，满足边缘AI应用对能效的严苛需求。

Method: 使用确定性、非参数化的双流形执行框架，将128维整数向量（数字化多频三角波形）通过8位有界变换矩阵映射到1024维超完备空间，经硬激活阈值生成超稀疏1比特二进制种群码（y ∈ {0,1}^1024）。通过硬件级数字低通滤波器消除高频几何噪声伪影。

Result: 硬件级数字低通滤波器完全消除了高频几何噪声伪影，将重构误差降低至近零水平；发现了算法复杂度悖论：低复杂度输入函数反而产生更高的重构误差。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了一种高稳定性、免乘法器的神经形态计算方案，可作为传统深度学习硬件的替代方案，适用于边缘AI应用。在τ=10和τ=100的分类阈值下均通过了实证评估验证。

Abstract: Energy-efficient neuromorphic computing requires alternative data-encoding paradigms that bypass power-hungry floating-point operations. This paper evaluates a deterministic, non-parametric dual-manifold execution framework that maps dense 128-element integer vectors - representing digitized multi-frequency trigonometric waveforms - into a 1024-dimensional overcomplete space using 8-bit bounded transformation matrices. By enforcing a hard activation threshold, the system yields an ultra-sparse, 1-bit binary population code where y belongs to the set (0, 1)^1024. We identify and address a critical phenotypic artifact of this non-parametric mapping: the emergence of high-frequency geometric noise during linear reconstruction. Furthermore, we document an algorithmic complexity paradox where low-complexity input functions yield significantly higher reconstruction errors than highly complex, high-degree trigonometric combinations. Because the underlying basis functions operate as purely objective mathematical entities without statistical priors regarding signal smoothness, this geometric noise is proven to be strictly orthogonal to the core signal topology. Consequently, we demonstrate that a low-overhead, hardware-level digital low-pass filter completely eliminates this artifact, reducing reconstruction errors to near-zero bounds even under tight overcompleteness constraints. This architecture validates a highly stable, multiplier-free alternative to traditional deep learning hardware for edge-AI applications, verified through comprehensive empirical evaluations across varying complexity scales and classification thresholds (tau = 10 and tau = 100).

</details>


### [146] [Toward Mitigating Process-Induced Performance Degradation in 3.5D Heterogeneous Packages via Pre-Silicon Firmware Co-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26176)
*Chi Fei Chung,Nikolai Nedovodin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出XRM-SSD V24/V7.0，一种面向Intel 3.5D异构集成封装的物理感知预测性固件调度层。通过90,000步LLM推理数据集的热电联合仿真，利用20-50 ms前瞻的主动负载密度热提示预置PowerVia电压轨，实现了热-负载相关性R²=0.9911、CPO光谱漂移低于0.36 nm、HBM漏电流钳制在1 MB/hr以下。V7.0扩展至多片架构并引入N×N热耦合矩阵与双极核。蒙特卡洛分析验证了工艺波动下的鲁棒性。预计可释放20-30%的计算能力并减少65-68%的EDA保护带。该工作展示了固件-硬件协同优化在缓解先进3.5D封装物理极限方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进3.5D异构集成封装（Foveros Direct 3D + PowerVia + EMIB-T + UCIe + HBM5）面临严重的热管理挑战和物理限制，传统的静态热管理方案无法有效应对动态工作负载下的热-电耦合问题，导致性能瓶颈和设计保护带过高。本文旨在通过固件-硬件协同优化的预测性调度策略，在硅前阶段探索缓解这些物理极限的可能性。

Method: 1) 构建XRM-SSD物理感知预测性固件调度层，包含20-50 ms前瞻的主动负载密度热提示机制；2) 利用90,000步LLM推理数据集进行详细热电联合仿真；3) 通过热提示预置PowerVia电压轨；4) V7.0扩展至多片架构，引入N×N热耦合矩阵和双极核；5) 采用蒙特卡洛分析（N=2,000次试验）评估工艺波动鲁棒性。

Result: 热-负载相关性R²=0.9911；CPO光谱漂移补偿后低于0.36 nm（TSMC公差预算的21%）；HBM漏电流在所有负载状态下钳制在1 MB/hr以下；蒙特卡洛分析验证工艺波动鲁棒性；预计释放20-30%的计算能力和减少65-68%的EDA保护带。

Conclusion: 固件-硬件协同优化是缓解先进3.5D封装中物理极限的有效方法。XRM-SSD通过预测性热管理实现了显著的热-电性能提升和设计保护带缩减，为下一代异构集成系统的热感知调度提供了可行方案。硅验证将在Intel 18A平台上进行。

Abstract: This paper presents a pre-silicon analysis of XRM-SSD V24/V7.0, a physics-aware predictive firmware scheduling layer for Intel's 3.5D heterogeneous integrated packages (Foveros Direct 3D + PowerVia + EMIB-T + UCIe + HBM5). Using detailed thermal-electrical co-simulation over a 90,000-step LLM inference dataset, we show that proactive workload-density-driven thermal hinting (20-50 ms look-ahead) enables pre-positioning of PowerVia voltage rails. Key results include a thermal-load correlation of R^2 = 0.9911, compensated CPO spectral drift below 0.36 nm (21% of TSMC tolerance budget), and HBM leakage current clamped below 1 MB/hr across all load states. Monte Carlo analysis (N=2,000 trials) confirms robustness under process variation. V7.0 extends the framework to multi-tile architectures with an N x N thermal coupling matrix and two-pole kernel. The approach demonstrates potential for 20-30% released compute and 65-68% EDA guard-band reduction. All metrics are engineering projections from pre-silicon characterization. Silicon validation on Intel 18A platforms is pending. This work highlights firmware-hardware co-optimization as an effective approach to mitigating physical limits in advanced 3.5D packaging.

</details>


### [147] [CVA6-RT: an Open-Source Time-Predictable RV64 Processor for Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26177)
*Enrico Zelioli,Christopher Reinwardt,Nils Wistoff,Robert Balas,Alessandro Ottaviano,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CVA6-RT 是 CVA6 核的实时微架构扩展，通过 TLB 分区与锁定、动态可配置的 L1 缓存便签模式、增强中断控制器及硬件辅助上下文堆叠，实现可预测地址翻译、确定性内存访问和低延迟中断处理，中断延迟仅 12 个周期，比基线 CVA6 低 10 倍，与 Arm Cortex-M 微控制器相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CVA6 核最差情况延迟不可控、任务时序执行变异性大，缺乏对实时执行的支持。

Method: 提出实时微架构扩展 CVA6-RT，实现 rv64gch ISA，包含 TLB 分区与锁定、L1 缓存可重构便签模式、增强中断控制器及硬件辅助上下文堆叠。

Result: 启用实时特性后，CVA6-RT 中断延迟仅 12 个周期，比基线 CVA6 核低 10 倍，与 Arm Cortex-M 微控制器相当。

Conclusion: CVA6-RT 通过多种实时微架构技术有效降低了中断延迟和时序变异性，适合实时嵌入式应用。

Abstract: This work presents CVA6-RT, a real-time micro-architectural extension of the CVA6 core to bound worst-case latency and reduce task's timing execution variability. CVA6-RT implements the rv64gch ISA and features advanced support for real-time execution, including TLB partitioning and locking for predictable address translation, a dynamically reconfigurable scratchpad mode in the L1 caches for deterministic memory access, and low-latency interrupt handling via an enhanced interrupt controller combined with hardware-assisted context stacking. With real-time features enabled, CVA6-RT achieves an interrupt latency of 12 cycles, comparable to that of simpler Arm Cortex-M microcontrollers, and 10x lower than the baseline CVA6 core.

</details>


### [148] [Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) for Hardware Security in Advanced Packaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26426)
*Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Pavanbabu Arjunamahanthi,M. Shafkat M. Khan,Liton Kumar Biswas,Nitin Varshney,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了将纳米机电系统（NEMS）作为新型硬件安全原语，利用其物理不可克隆函数、形状记忆材料、共振指纹和物理解锁架构等机制，在器件层面实现物理保障、篡改检测和身份认证，提供比传统数字安全方法更鲁棒、低功耗的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造和先进封装领域硬件安全威胁日益严重（如篡改、伪造、供应链渗透），需要新型物理机制来应对复杂攻击。

Method: 采用NEMS基物理不可克隆函数（PUF）、形状记忆材料、基于共振的指纹识别以及物理解锁架构等多种机制，利用机械不可预测性和制造诱导的纳米级变异性来实现安全功能。

Result: 增强了对抗逆向工程、侧信道攻击和环境退化的鲁棒性，提供了物理鲁棒且低功耗的替代方案，可与标准半导体工艺流程无缝集成。

Conclusion: NEMS技术为国防、航空航天、关键基础设施和消费电子等领域提供了可扩展、可验证且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: As hardware security threats escalate across semiconductor manufacturing and advanced packaging, there is a growing need for novel physical mechanisms to counter sophisticated attacks such as tampering, counterfeiting, and supply chain infiltration. This paper presents Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) as an emerging class of hardware security primitives that enable physical assurance, tamper detection, and authentication at the device level. Leveraging mechanisms such as NEMS-based Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs), shape memory materials, resonance-based fingerprints, and physical unlocking architectures, these systems offer enhanced resilience to reverse engineering, side-channel attacks, and environmental degradation. By harnessing mechanical unpredictability and fabrication-induced nanoscale variability, NEMS technologies introduce a physically robust and low-power alternative to conventional digital security methods. Their seamless integration into standard semiconductor workflows paves the way for scalable, verifiable, and secure solutions across defense, aerospace, critical infrastructure, and consumer electronics.

</details>


### [149] [GRAINS: Storage-Aware Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design Enabling High-Performance and Low-Cost Graph-Based Genome Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26468)
*Nika Mansouri Ghiasi,Harun Mustafa,Talu Güloglu,Rakesh Nadig,Konstantina Koliogeorgi,Susana Rebolledo Ruiz,Marc Rautmann,Furkan Eris,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Jisung Park,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GRAINS是首个在存储设备内部进行大规模基因组图谱分析的系统，通过存储感知的算法-架构协同设计，利用存储内和闪存内处理技术，实现了相比最先进基线2.7-47.8倍加速和4.4-31.6倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基因组图谱分析面临严重的数据移动开销，现有工具无法在存储系统内部高效运行（受限于SSD内部资源），而先前针对线性基因组分析或非基因组图分析的存储中心系统也不适用于基因组图谱的独特数据结构和访问模式。

Method: GRAINS采用存储感知的算法-架构协同设计，包括三个关键方面：(1)基于基因组图谱独特特征提出新的批处理和执行流程；(2)通过闪存内和存储内处理避免传输低重用的闪存页；(3)通过改造现有SSD结构设计轻量级调度技术以充分利用闪存芯片的并行性。

Result: 相比最先进软件基线，GRAINS实现2.7-47.8倍加速和4.4-31.6倍能耗降低；相比硬件加速基线，实现1.5-17.0倍加速和3.1-20.7倍能耗降低。

Conclusion: 存储内处理是解决大规模基因组图谱分析数据移动开销的根本方案，通过算法-架构协同设计可有效克服SSD内部资源限制，实现显著的性能提升和能耗优化。

Abstract: Graph-based representations of genome sequences have emerged as a powerful approach for representing massive genomic databases in an expressive and efficient way. Despite their benefits, analysis on large-scale genome graphs incurs significant data movement overhead from the storage system due to accessing large amounts of low-reuse data. Processing data directly inside the storage device can be a fundamental solution for mitigating this overhead. However, none of the existing tools for graph-based genome analysis can be efficiently used inside the storage system due to the limited internal hardware resources in modern SSDs. At the same time, prior storage-centric systems developed for (i) traditional, linear non-graph-based genome analysis or (ii) conventional, non-genomic graph analysis are not suitable for the unique data structures and access patterns of graph-based genome analysis.
  We propose GRAINS, the first system for analysis with large-scale genome graphs in storage. Through our detailed examination of typical analysis pipelines that operate on genome graphs, we perform storage-aware algorithm-architecture co-design to (i) make these pipelines more storage-friendly and (ii) further improve performance, energy-efficiency, and cost via in-storage and in-flash processing. GRAINS's co-design is based on three key aspects. First, we propose a new batching and execution flow, based on unique features of genome graphs. Second, via in-flash and in-storage processing, we avoid transferring low-reused flash pages. Third, to leverage the full parallelism of flash dies, we design an effective, yet lightweight, scheduling technique, enabled by re-purposing the existing SSD structures. GRAINS provides 2.7x-47.8x speedup (4.4x-31.6x energy reduction) over the state-of-the-art software baselines, and 1.5x-17.0x speedup (3.1x-20.7x energy reduction) over a hardware-accelerated baseline.

</details>


### [150] [SegFold: Accelerating Sparse GEMM with a Fine-Grained Dynamic Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26701)
*Xinrui Wu,Hanyu Wang,Jason Cong,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出动态数据流Segment用于稀疏矩阵乘法(SpGEMM)，并设计对应加速器SegFold，通过细粒度动态调度和动态重映射实现数据复用和负载均衡，相比最先进的静态数据流加速器实现1.95倍加速，相比最佳静态数据流配置实现5.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPU、GPU及专用加速器均采用静态数据流（如内积、外积、Gustavson等），每种静态数据流都会牺牲部分数据复用机会并对负载均衡施加约束，导致效率损失。

Method: 在典型SpGEMM数据流中引入动态性：1) 细粒度动态调度，优化数据复用并减少资源竞争；2) 动态重映射部分完成的工作，改善负载均衡和并行度。形式化为Segment数据流，并协同设计SegFold加速器，包含内存控制器（在局部窗口中识别细粒度复用机会）和合并网络（将输入路由到合适的PE并动态重映射工作分配）。

Result: 在不同密度和矩阵规模下，SegFold相比最先进的SpGEMM加速器实现几何平均1.95倍加速，相比最佳静态数据流配置实现5.3倍加速。

Conclusion: 在数据流设计空间中引入动态性可以解锁单纯静态调度无法实现的复用和负载均衡收益。

Abstract: Generalized sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) is critical in many domains. Existing CPUs and GPUs, as well as specialized accelerators, rely on static dataflows (e.g., inner product, outer product, Gustavson, etc.). Each static dataflow sacrifices some data reuse opportunities and imposes constraints on load balance.
  To address this inefficiency, we extend the typical SpGEMM dataflows by considering dynamism. Specifically, we add fine-grained dynamic scheduling to optimize reuse and reduce resource contention. We also develop dynamic remapping of partially completed work to improve load balance and parallelism. These ideas are formalized into a specific dataflow called Segment. To demonstrate Segment, we codesign a SpGEMM accelerator called SegFold. SegFold includes a memory controller that identifies fine-grained reuse opportunities in a local window of the stationary input array and exploits them through dynamic work assignment. It also incorporates a merge network that routes inputs to appropriate processing elements (PEs) for computation while dynamically remapping the work assigned to each PE to balance load.
  Across diverse densities and matrix sizes, SegFold achieves a geometric-mean $1.95\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art SpGEMM accelerators and $5.3\times$ over the best static dataflow configuration, demonstrating that adding dynamism to the dataflow design space unlocks reuse and load-balance gains that no static scheduling choice can achieve in isolation.

</details>


### [151] [Residual GPU Cache State on Apple M4 Pro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27098)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在Apple M4 Pro上通过同步Metal实验，量化了GPU命令完成后CPU缓存被置换的现象，发现首次CPU遍历因GPU大足迹而变慢，第二次遍历可恢复大部分性能，并提出了简单的软件恢复机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon的统一CPU-GPU内存架构中，GPU命令完成后的缓存状态未被文档化，理解这一相边界行为对优化CPU-GPU协同工作至关重要。

Method: 在14核Apple M4 Pro上，使用8192字节系统级缓存占用模式适配同步Metal实验，先让GPU内核访问0-512 MiB数据，再启动16 MiB CPU探测，对比首次与第二次遍历的延迟差异；同时通过匹配块实验测量高优先级CPU流量下的GPU减速，并结合Root PMU和IOReport直方图进行硬件层验证。

Result: 首次CPU遍历在GPU大足迹后显著变慢，第二次遍历可消除大部分额外开销；GPU在后台QoS下接近基线性能；PMU和IOReport数据区分了L1D refill扇区与软件缓存行大小，暴露了页偏移相关的冲突行为，并分离了P核、E核和AGX的访存需求。

Conclusion: M4 Pro上存在可复现的GPU后缓存置换窗口，且可通过单次软件遍历有效恢复，为统一内存架构下的CPU-GPU调度优化提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Apple silicon exposes unified CPU-GPU memory, but the cache state left after a completed GPU command is not documented. This paper characterizes that phase boundary on a 14-core Apple M4 Pro. We validate the measurement pipeline against unmodified STREAM 5.10 and BabelStream 5.0, then adapt an 8192-byte system-level-cache occupancy pattern to a synchronized Metal experiment. A GPU kernel touches 0 to 512 MiB and finishes before a 16 MiB CPU probe begins. The first CPU traversal is slower after large GPU footprints, while a second traversal removes most of the cost, showing residual shared-cache displacement rather than simultaneous DRAM contention. A separate matched-block experiment measures GPU slowdown under high-priority CPU traffic and finds background QoS close to baseline. Root PMU measurements and public IOReport histograms provide hardware grounding: they distinguish L1D refill sectors from software cache-line size, expose page-offset-dependent conflict behavior, and separate performance-core, efficiency-core, and AGX demand. The results identify a reproducible post-GPU cache-displacement window on M4 Pro and quantify a simple one-pass software recovery mechanism.

</details>


### [152] [Evaluating Architectural Trade-offs in CGRAs: The Impact of Scratchpad Memory and Heterogeneity on Compute-Intensive Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27240)
*María José Belda,Lara Orlandic,Fernando Castro,Miguel Peón-Quirós,Katzalin Olcoz,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了CGRA架构中处理单元异构性和本地数据重用支持对边缘计算工作负载（ViT、FFT、GEMM）的影响，发现SPM可将内存流量降低8倍，同构架构面积开销比最先进CGRA小4.4-8.2倍且运行频率达700MHz，为边缘场景下的CGRA架构选择提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算应用（特别是高吞吐量流处理的ViT）需要在严苛的功耗和面积约束下实现大规模空间并行性和高效数据移动，CGRA虽能平衡性能、灵活性和能效，但不同设计选择（处理单元异构性、本地数据重用支持）的影响尚需系统分析。

Method: 论文通过评估FFT、GEMM以及端到端的癫痫检测Transformer工作负载，对比了两种CGRA配置：基线同构架构和集成了专用功能单元与Scratchpad Memory (SPM)的异构架构。

Result: SPM将内存流量降低8倍；异构架构在数据洗牌任务中能效更优；同构架构面积开销比最先进CGRA小4.4-8.2倍；同构架构维持700MHz频率，在矩阵计算中比异构配置快5倍。

Conclusion: 这项工作为根据边缘规模工作负载的算术强度、性能目标和资源限制选择CGRA架构提供了设计路线图。

Abstract: Modern edge computing applications, particularly high-throughput stream processing like Vision Transformers (ViTs), demand massive spatial parallelism and efficient data movement under tight power and area constraints. Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Architectures (CGRAs) offer a promising paradigm to balance performance, flexibility, and energy efficiency. This paper analyzes the impact of two critical CGRA design choices: processing element heterogeneity and local data reuse support. We evaluate essential computational kernels (Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and General Matrix Multiply (GEMM)) alongside an end-to-end seizure detection transformer workload across two distinct configurations: a baseline homogeneous architecture and a heterogeneous evolution integrating specialized functional units with an Scratchpad Memory (SPM). Our evaluation demonstrates that the SPM significantly optimizes data movement, reducing memory traffic eightfold compared to a memory-less design. While the heterogeneous architecture achieves superior energy efficiency for data-shuffling tasks, the homogeneous design minimizes area overhead by 4.4x to 8.2x relative to state-of-the-art CGRAs. Furthermore, it sustains a 700 MHz operating frequency, enabling up to a 5x execution speedup over the heterogeneous configuration during matrix computations. Ultimately, this work provides an architectural roadmap for selecting CGRA fabrics based on the arithmetic intensity, performance goals, and resource envelopes of edge-scale workloads.

</details>


### [153] [CHIA: An open-source framework for principled, agentic AI-driven hardware/software co-design research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27350)
*Angela Cui,Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Raghav Gupta,Jim Fang,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Ella Schwarz,Junha Kim,Yakun Sophia Shao,Borivoje Nikolic,Christopher W. Fletcher,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHIA 是一个开源硬件/软件协同设计框架，将 AI 驱动的设计流程表达为有向循环图（CHIA loops），支持多种工具集成、故障隔离、大规模实验，并通过五个案例展示了其在敏捷、可扩展的 AI 辅助协同设计研究中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 AI 在硬件/软件协同设计中有巨大潜力，但目前的应用通常局限于小规模问题的孤立场景，缺乏一个能够敏捷、可扩展地构建和部署复杂 AI 驱动开发工作流的通用框架。

Method: 提出 CHIA 框架，将协同设计流程建模为有向循环图（CHIA loops），节点可执行芯片设计工具、模拟器、AI 模型、进化编码代理等；提供 CHIA 库封装多种流行工具；支持 AI 模型与硬件工具隔离、故障容忍、大规模异构系统部署。

Result: 通过五个案例验证：RTL-to-gem5 模拟器自动对齐、LLM 驱动的微架构 RTL 实现、IPC 感知的关键路径优化、进化式架构探索、可维护的代理式 GitHub 问题修复。

Conclusion: CHIA 为 AI 辅助的硬件/软件协同设计研究提供了一个开源、可扩展、注重可复现科学的框架，有望加速该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence shows great promise for radically improving the pace of innovation in hardware/software co-design research across computer architecture, systems, compilers, and VLSI. Thus far, however, applications of AI in these contexts have generally been demonstrated in isolated settings on small-scale problems, due to the difficulty of designing and deploying complex AI-infused hardware and software development workflows.
  This paper introduces CHIA, an open-source hardware/software co-design framework for agile and principled research on the application of AI to co-design. CHIA treats the productive construction and scalable deployment of the co-design flow itself as a first-class objective. In CHIA, agentic AI-driven hardware and software design flows are expressed as \textit{CHIA loops}: directed cyclic graphs whose nodes execute various system-on-chip design tools, microarchitectural simulators, software build systems, AI models, evolutionary coding agents, and more. The \textit{CHIA library} provides node implementations for many popular tools, including Chipyard, gem5, ChampSim, FireSim, Hammer (thus several commercial ASIC CAD tools), Vivado, AlphaEvolve, AdaEvolve, and many others.
  CHIA also provides a broad set of features to conduct principled science around these flows. These include isolation between AI models and hardware tools, profiling mechanisms, fault-tolerant execution, and reliability at scale across hundreds of heterogeneous systems (CPUs, FPGAs, GPUs, etc., across public cloud/on-prem.).
  To showcase CHIA, we present five CHIA loops as case studies: (1) automatic RTL-to-gem5 simulator alignment, (2) LLM-driven implementation of microarchitectural features in RTL, (3) agentic, IPC-aware critical path optimization, (4) evolutionary architectural discovery, and (5) maintainer-friendly agentic GitHub issue fixing.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络代理模型，用于高效、准确地预测Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的相分离微观结构演化，在长时间滚动预测中保持稳定性和守恒性，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性偏微分方程描述的系统时空演化在物理、化学和生物领域广泛存在，传统数值求解器计算成本高，需要高效的替代模型。

Method: 基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为代理模型，用于学习Cahn-Hilliard方程控制的二元混合物相分离微观结构演化。

Result: 预测在长时间滚动中保持稳定和准确，适用于临界和非临界混合物；能保持混合物的成分守恒；准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律。

Conclusion: 该框架对具有保守动力学的系统建模有效，并可扩展到其他复杂动力学系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [155] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHISAO，一种GPU原生的种群优化器，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环和冻结确认模式，在42个基准函数上实现100%模式恢复，速度提升最高达39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态黑箱函数求模方法（如basin-hopping、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）均为顺序执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行能力。

Method: CHISAO采用GPU原生并行种群优化，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环：达到真正峰值的样本被冻结（"stuck"）保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索，并采用两种自适应重播种策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）维持种群多样性。

Result: 在SFU优化基准套件的所有42个函数上（维度2-64），CHISAO实现100%模式恢复，而CPU基线在d≥8的最难多模态函数上全部失效；速度提升最高达39倍（无梯度信息的最坏情况），在噪声环境下仍保持100%可靠。

Conclusion: CHISAO证明了GPU原生并行优化策略在多模态黑箱函数求模问题上的显著优势，以开源Python包形式发布。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [156] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小认知视角出发，将单细胞藻类的趋光性重述为一种主动信息驱动的感觉运动过程。通过POMDP与CRN-ODE框架建模，结合逆强化学习从实验轨迹推断行为目标，发现run-tumble交替是一种信息获取策略，用于解决感测模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的run-tumble趋光模型仅将藻类行为描述为刺激-响应的机械过程，忽略了生物体如何主动采样环境以减少感觉不确定性。作者希望从最小认知视角重新理解细胞导航中的信息驱动行为。

Method: 提出了一个将POMDP与生化反应动力学（CRN-ODE）结合的框架：隐藏环境变量通过无记忆贝叶斯更新维持最小内部状态；包含光感受的生物物理观察过程及化学可计算的信息增益多项式上界；使用逆强化学习从30条衣藻实验轨迹推断行为目标，并以SSA为基线进行基准测试。

Result: 模型能够再现经验性的向光对齐分布，表现与目标SSA基线相当。Run-tumble交替被揭示为信息获取策略：tumble通过重定向细胞以采样新的感觉配置，从而解决感测模糊性。

Conclusion: 细胞内生化网络可以支持适应性信息寻求行为，趋光导航中的run-tumble交替不仅是机械响应，更是一种主动降低感官不确定性的认知策略。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [157] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经网络架构搜索（NAS）在生成对抗网络（GAN）设计中的应用，分类比较了不同搜索策略、评估指标和性能表现，指出进化算法与梯度方法的优势，强调了超越IS和FID的稳健评估指标的重要性，并识别了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS作为一种自动化架构设计技术，能够解决GAN手工设计中存在的挑战，提升GAN的性能、稳定性和效率。本文旨在通过系统综述，为研究人员开发更有效的NAS方法提供指导，推动GAN领域发展。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，基于搜索策略、评估指标和性能结果等标准，对应用于GAN的各类NAS方法进行分类和比较分析。

Result: 发现进化算法和基于梯度的方法在特定场景下表现优越；指出传统IS和FID分数之外，需要更稳健的评估指标；强调评估GAN性能时需要使用多样化数据集。

Conclusion: NAS在提升GAN性能、稳定性和效率方面具有显著优势，但现有方法仍存在局限性，未来研究需在评估指标、数据集多样性和搜索策略等方面进一步突破。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [158] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE是一个神经符号框架，通过将WHO突变知识图谱作为结构化生物学约束集成到神经基因组模型中，利用可学习的认知信任门动态加权神经证据与符号生物学知识，在结核分枝杆菌数据集上实现了高精度AMR预测（异烟肼AUROC 0.9760），并引入生物学基础比率（BGR）指标量化神经归因与已知生物学的一致性，达到92.5%的符号覆盖率和可验证的临床审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于WGS的AMR预测模型虽然精度高，但缺乏将神经归因建立在既定生物学通路上的机制，导致预测缺乏可解释性和临床可信度。

Method: 提出KG-TRACE框架，将WHO突变知识图谱（KG）以RotatE嵌入形式与神经基因组特征通过可学习的认知信任门动态融合，引入生物学基础比率（BGR）作为量化神经归因与生物学知识对齐程度的数据集级指标。

Result: 在CRyPTIC结核分枝杆菌队列上，异烟肼AUROC达0.9760，预测的符号覆盖率为92.5%，能有效识别MDR共现伪影，并为'UNCERTAIN'病例生成实验室随访标记。

Conclusion: 神经符号基础提供了可验证的临床审计轨迹，弥合了预测准确性与临床信任之间的差距，其核心价值在于符号基础而非预测性能提升。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [159] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM作为评估器（grader）时，温度参数设置为0并不能保证评估结果的确定性。在真实安全评估代码库中，由于API默认温度参数为1.0以及即使强制贪心解码仍存在非确定性，接近决策边界的项目会出现高达50%的通过/失败翻转。作者建议将评估器分歧作为一等健康指标纳入评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广泛存在的假设——将评估器的采样温度设为0即可实现确定性评分——缺乏实证检验。安全评估中的通过/失败判决直接影响模型部署决策，其不确定性可能导致严重的安全隐患。

Method: 使用日本AISI的开源安全评估代码库aisev，对690次API调用（涵盖两家提供商、三个模型层级、五种采样配置）进行实验，比较默认温度、temperature=0以及强制贪心解码（top_k=1）条件下的评分一致性。

Result: 1) 默认温度1.0时，接近决策边界的项目在20次运行中出现最高50%的通过/失败翻转；2) 即使设置temperature=0，7个边界项目中仍有1-2个不可重现；3) Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已弃用temperature参数，使得主要缓解措施无法应用于新模型。

Conclusion: 评估框架报告单次运行结果而不提供方差或评估器分歧指标，会将噪声呈现为安全属性。应将评估器分歧作为与评分同等重要的一等健康指标。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [160] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue2Group框架，从初始线索出发通过分析师交互逐步恢复洗钱团伙，解决了现有图异常检测方法与实际反洗钱调查流程不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法主要产生节点级风险告警，而全局团伙发现方法被动搜索整个网络，两者均不匹配实际反洗钱调查中从具体线索出发逐步扩展调查的流程。

Method: 提出Clue2Group框架：1）构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以降低噪声并保留链状和环状洗钱结构；2）使用多语义局部时序GNN估计线索条件化的局部风险场；3）整合风险、结构及先验模式证据以恢复连贯的洗钱团伙。

Result: 在两个大规模反洗钱基准数据集上验证了Clue2Group的有效性，提供了实用的线索驱动分析框架。

Conclusion: Clue2Group为图反洗钱研究与实际调查工作流之间架起桥梁，是向实用化反洗钱分析迈出的可行一步。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [161] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联邦哈希投影潜在因子模型(FHPLF)，通过二值化梯度矩阵、投影汉明距离和隐私增强上传策略，在联邦学习框架下实现高效、准确且隐私保护的哈希学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需要集中存储数据，违反数据隐私法规；联邦学习虽能保护数据但通信开销大且存在梯度泄露风险；现有哈希方法二进制编码表达能力有限，影响模型精度。

Method: (a) 用二值梯度矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算/存储/通信成本并增强隐私；(b) 采用投影汉明距离建模相似性，捕捉每个比特的重要性以提升表示能力；(c) 提出安全二值梯度重组与隐私增强上传策略(SBG-PEU)，降低传输过程中的交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，FHPLF在精度、效率和隐私保护之间取得了优越的平衡，持续优于最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: FHPLF通过二值化与投影距离等创新有效解决了联邦哈希学习中二进制编码表达能力受限的问题，实现了隐私、效率与精度的协同优化。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [162] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代机器学习系统已从孤立预测模型演变为复杂的社会技术架构，积极调节人类机会。算法公平性领域虽已兴起，但早期缓解策略存在两个根本局限：依赖确定性点估计进行审计，以及将个体视为缺乏结构背景的孤立实体。本论文旨在识别并解决这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统已超越纯粹的预测工具，成为调节社会资源分配的社会技术架构，但其深度嵌入结构性不平等和偏见。现有算法公平性缓解策略因过度简化而效果有限，亟需突破。

Method: 通过批判性分析识别当代公平范式的两大局限：1) 依赖确定性点估计进行审计；2) 将个体视为脱离结构背景的孤立实体。

Result: 指出现有公平性方法在复杂社会技术环境中有效性受限的两个根本原因。

Conclusion: 需要超越确定性点估计和个体孤立假设，发展更具社会技术语境敏感性的公平性框架。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [163] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了拓扑数据分析（TDA）在洪水检测中的应用，利用SEN12-FLOOD数据集提取拓扑特征，证明拓扑描述符能够独立携带洪水信号，并增强现有神经网络的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洪水检测方法（如CNN+GRU、ViT）虽性能提升，但模型是黑箱，难以解释决策边界和内部推理，尤其在安全关键的遥感领域。

Method: 从SEN12-FLOOD数据集的图像中提取拓扑特征（TDA），将这些拓扑描述符融入神经网络，与现有网络（如ResNet-50）结合进行洪水检测。

Result: 拓扑描述符能独立携带有效的洪水信号，且与现有网络互补，提升了洪水检测系统的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: TDA为洪水检测提供了数学上严谨的可解释性框架，能够有效弥补深度学习黑箱模型的不足，是遥感灾害监测中有前景的方向。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>


### [164] [A General Framework for Learning Algebraic Properties from Cayley Graphs using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26212)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与具体代数性质无关的图神经网络框架，直接从Cayley图中学习有限群的可交换性、幂零性和可解性等代数性质，实验表明该框架能够成功地从图表示中提取出丰富的代数信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前针对可解性预测的GNN方法推广为通用的、与代数性质无关的学习框架，探究能否从Cayley图表示中直接恢复有限群的多种代数结构信息。

Method: 采用统一的GNN架构和训练流程，以有限群的Cayley图为输入，分别预测群的可交换性、幂零性和可解性等代数性质，在多个群族的数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在多个有限群族上的实验结果表明，该框架能够成功学习和区分不同的代数性质，证明了Cayley图编码了丰富的代数信息。

Conclusion: 图表示中蕴含了可被GNN提取的代数结构信息，该工作为将图表示学习应用于有限群代数性质研究提供了概念验证。

Abstract: A Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for predicting the solvability of finite groups from their Cayley graph representations was introduced in [1]. In the present work, we generalize this approach and develop a property-independent framework for learning algebraic properties of finite groups directly from Cayley graphs. As representative case studies, we consider abelianity, nilpotency, and solvability. Using a common GNN architecture and training pipeline, we investigate the extent to which algebraic structure can be recovered from graph-based representations alone. Results on a collection of finite groups drawn from several families demonstrate that the framework successfully learns and distinguishes multiple algebraic properties from their associated Cayley graphs. These findings suggest that substantial algebraic information is encoded in graph representations and can be extracted through GNNs. More broadly, the proposed framework provides a proof of concept for applying graph representation learning to the study of algebraic properties of finite groups.

</details>


### [165] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个自动从PyTorch/JAX源码推导理论最小执行时间（Speed-of-Light bound）的框架，利用LLM翻译代码为中间表示，再通过确定性流程计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的SOL界限，支持多保真度分析，在多种负载上验证了零违反率，并展示了余量分析、优化识别、跨平台探索和硬件配置四个用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOL分析依赖手动推导，容易出错且无法跟上快速模型开发节奏；缺乏自动化工具来计算深度学习模型在特定硬件上的理论最小执行时间，导致优化工作缺乏明确上界指引。

Method: SOLAR包含三阶段流程：1) LLM前端将PyTorch/JAX源码翻译为可执行的Affine Loop IR，通过输出对比验证；2) 确定性流程将IR提升为einsum图；3) 分析后端计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的SOL界限。结合生成式（LLM）和确定性组件实现自动化。

Result: 在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人负载上验证，零SOL违反率；提供多保真度分析能力，有效识别优化机会，支持跨平台探索和逆roofline硬件配置。

Conclusion: SOLAR实现了SOL分析的完全自动化，覆盖多种算子与语言，验证准确率高，为软硬件协同优化提供了实用的性能上界分析工具。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [166] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fast-LeWM通过动作前缀并行预测替代传统逐步自回归展开，在减少规划时间的同时提升规划成功率和长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统JEPA类世界模型（如LeWM）在视觉规划中依赖逐帧自回归展开来评估动作序列，导致计算成本高且随规划视野增长累积潜在误差。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出Fast-LeWM，将动作前缀作为基本预测单元，对候选动作序列的各个前缀进行编码，并行预测执行这些前缀后到达的未来隐状态，替代传统的单步隐状态转移模型的重复滚动。

Result: 在多任务上，Fast-LeWM相比LeWM提升了平均成功率，显著降低了规划时间，且开环潜在损失增长率随规划视野增加而明显减缓。

Conclusion: 通过前缀级监督学习状态在多视野下的连续演化，Fast-LeWM在保持无需重建优势的同时，大幅提升了视觉世界模型在规划任务中的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>


### [167] [Dataset Usage Inference without Shadow Models or Held-out Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26257)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak,Jan Dubiński,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需影子模型和真实保留数据的实用数据集使用推断（DUI）框架，通过生成合成非成员样本、提取多样成员信号并将DUI转化为混合比例估计问题，来估计数据集中多大比例被用于训练目标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DUI方法依赖不切实际的假设（需要训练昂贵的影子模型模仿目标模型，且需要已知的训练样本和确认未参与训练的分布内保留集），使得这些方法在当代大模型和真实数据所有权争议中不实用。

Method: 提出实用DUI框架：1）生成合成非成员样本替代真实保留数据；2）提取多样的成员关系信号；3）将DUI问题转化为混合比例估计问题，从而估计候选数据集中被用于训练的比例。

Result: 在大规模图像生成模型上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地量化数据集使用情况。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据所有者提供了一个实用工具，用于确定他们的数据在多大程度上被用于训练模型，解决了现有DUI方法在实践中的关键限制。

Abstract: How much of my data was used to train a machine learning model? Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. However, existing DUI methods rely on assumptions that rarely hold in practice: they require training expensive shadow models to imitate the target model, and they assume access to both known training samples and an in-distribution held-out set confirmed to be absent from training. These conditions make current approaches impractical for modern large models and real data ownership disputes. We introduce a practical DUI framework that removes these constraints. Our method requires neither shadow models nor real held-out data. Instead, it generates synthetic non-member samples, extracts diverse membership signals, and casts DUI as a mixture proportion estimation problem to estimate what share of the candidate dataset was used during training. Experiments on large image generative models show that our method reliably quantifies dataset usage, providing a practical tool for data owners to determine how much of their data was used to train a model.

</details>


### [168] [Equivariance and Augmentation for Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26273)
*Miaowen Dong,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中数据增强对等变性的影响，推导了变分推断下达到精确等变的条件，给出了等变误差界，并提出三种新的对称化技术，其中轨道扩展方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管等变网络在理论上已被充分研究，但数据增强（另一种实现对称性的方式）的理论分析较少，因为需要控制训练动力学。受增强无限深度集成可达到精确等变的最新结果启发，本文旨在研究BNN中数据增强对等变性的影响。

Method: 聚焦于指数族变分分布，推导精确等变的条件；推导等变误差界；提出三种对称化技术（包括轨道扩展）；通过大量数值实验验证方法效果。

Result: 所提轨道扩展方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 数据增强结合适当的对称化技术可以有效提升BNN的等变性，轨道扩展是一种实用且有效的对称化方法。

Abstract: Symmetries are important for many deep learning tasks, ranging from applications in the sciences to medical imaging. However, there is an ongoing debate about whether to impose symmetry constraints on the neural network architecture (yielding equivariant neural networks) or learn them from augmented training data. Although equivariant networks are well-studied theoretically, much less is known about data augmentation, since analyzing augmentation requires control over the training dynamics. Inspired by recent results that show that augmented infinite deep ensembles are exactly equivariant, we study data augmentation for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) trained with variational inference. We focus on variational distributions in the exponential family and derive conditions under which exact equivariance is reached. We furthermore obtain bounds on the equivariance error and introduce three novel symmetrization techniques which boost the effect of data augmentation in this setting. We conduct extensive numerical experiments which show that one of our symmetrization methods (orbit expansion) outperforms the baseline in both equivariance and overall performance. Our code is available at github.com/dmw1998/augment-BNNs

</details>


### [169] [SSM Adapters via Hankel Reduced-order Modeling: Injection Site Determines Task Suitability in Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26290)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HRM适配器，一种基于状态空间模型（SSM）的参数高效微调方法，通过Hankel平衡截断初始化，利用FFT实现并行扫描，在计算复杂度和LoRA持平的情况下，在长文本任务上显著超越LoRA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参数高效微调（PEFT）在需要序列状态累积的任务中，能否从SSM适配器中获益，以及MLP层是否比注意力层更适合作为注入点。现有PEFT（如LoRA）主要针对注意力投影，对状态跟踪类任务效果有限。

Method: 提出HRM适配器：一种基于SSM的残差模块，通过经验Hankel格拉姆矩阵的平衡截断（Balanced Truncation）初始化。利用系统矩阵Ã的时间不变性，设计基于FFT的精确并行扫描算法，确保在所有上下文长度上与LoRA计算复杂度一致。

Result: 在Mistral-7B上（8.4M可训练参数），HRM在LongBench任务中优于LoRA变体：QuALITY相对准确率提升34.8%，QMSum相对ROUGE-1提升71.6%。在18种合成状态追踪任务（DFA、Parity）和字符级语言建模（enwik8）上表现一致优越。

Conclusion: 门控分析表明HRM适配器能有效学习调节循环机制，为长上下文序列建模提供了比低秩适配更稳健的架构替代方案。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) typically targets attention projectors, its efficacy for tasks requiring sequential state accumulation remains under-explored. We examine if PEFT for such tasks can benefit from state space model (SSMs) adapters, and if MLP blocks are better injection sites. We introduce Hankel Reduced order Model (HRM) adapter, an SSM-based residual module initialized via Balanced Truncation of empirical Hankel Grammians. By leveraging the time-invariance of the system matrix $\bar{A}$, HRM enables an exact FFT-based parallel scan, achieving computational parity with LoRA across all context lengths. In iso-parametric evaluations on Mistral-7B (8.4M trainable parameters), HRM outperforms LoRA variants on LongBench tasks, including QuALITY (+34.8\% relative accuracy) and QMSum (+71.6\% relative ROUGE-1). HRM further demonstrates consistent superiority across 18 configurations of synthetic state-tracking (DFA, Parity) and character-level language modeling (enwik8). Gate analysis reveals that HRM adapters effectively learn to modulate recurrence, providing a robust architectural alternative to low-rank adaptation for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [170] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出红皇后哥德尔机（RQGM），一个在非平稳效用下进行递归自我改进的演化框架，通过分阶段固定评估标准并允许跨阶段效用演化，在编码、科学论文写作评审和奥赛级证明任务上超越现有自我改进智能体，同时纠正了基线评审者对AI生成论文的过度接受偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体假设评估标准是固定不变的（如固定的验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了演化中环境与物种共同变化的核心特征。本文旨在将评估纳入改进循环，使搜索能面向演化的评估者、对抗性目标和动态效用，超越静态基准的限制。

Method: 提出RQGM框架，将搜索组织为多个时期，每个时期内评估标准固定，时期边界处可更新效用函数。通过这种受控的效用演化，每个时期内的自我改进保证仍然成立，同时目标可以跨时期演化。

Result: 1) 在可验证编码任务上，RQGM通过添加智能体作为评审的代码审查信号，将测试通过率提升至超过先前SOTA，且节省1.35-1.72倍token；2) 在科学论文写作和评审中，共演化的写作者在多样化评审面板下接受率提升1.78-1.86倍，共演化的评审者准确率提升9%；3) RQGM通过对抗性目标纠正了基线评审者对AI生成论文过度接受（高达1.91倍人类率）的偏差。

Conclusion: 将评估纳入递归自我改进循环是实现更真实演化式智能的关键，RQGM通过受控效用演化有效实现了这一目标，在多项任务上超越静态评估的自我改进方法，并解决了AI评估中的系统性偏差问题。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [171] [High-Probability PL-SGD with Markovian Noise: Optimal Mixing and Tail Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26316)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件下，梯度样本由外生马尔可夫链生成时的一阶优化方法。在轻尾设定下，弥合了SGD的高概率界与期望界之间的差距，证明了对混合时间的线性依赖是最优的；在重尾设定下，设计了全样本裁剪分块方法，并建立了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻尾设定下SGD的高概率边界为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$，与期望边界$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$存在一个混合时间因子的差距。本文旨在弥合这一差距，并进一步扩展到重尾马尔可夫梯度场景。

Method: 轻尾：提出滞后分块(lag-blocking)论证，建立统一的高概率保证，随机项为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$；重尾：设计全样本裁剪分块(all-samples clipped block)方法，利用所有马尔可夫转移同时减轻马尔可夫偏差。

Result: 轻尾：证明了$\Omega(\sigma^2 t_{mix}/k)$的下界，说明对混合时间的线性依赖是最优的；重尾：实现了高概率随机误差$\widetilde{O}(\sigma_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$，并通过粘性马尔可夫链建立了匹配下界。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了轻尾PL-SGD中混合时间的最优多项式依赖关系，以及鲁棒机制下重尾指数和有效样本量的最优依赖关系。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for smooth objectives satisfying the Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) condition when gradient samples are generated by an exogenous Markov chain. In the light-tailed setting, prior uniform-in-time high-probability bounds for ordinary Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) under a standard growth envelope scale as $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$, leaving a gap with the $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ expectation bounds. We close this gap using a lag-blocking argument to establish a uniform high-probability guarantee with a leading stochastic term of $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$ under geometric mixing. We prove this linear dependence on the mixing time is optimal via a matching $Ω(σ^2 t_{mix}/k)$ lower bound on a quadratic objective driven by a persistent two-state chain.
  We then extend this framework to heavy-tailed Markovian gradients satisfying a stationary finite-$p$-moment condition, $p \in (1,2]$. We design an all-samples clipped block method that uses every Markov transition while mitigating Markovian bias. Under a transition budget $T$, this algorithm achieves a high-probability stochastic error of $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$. We establish a matching lower bound by reducing PL optimization to heavy-tailed mean estimation for a sticky Markov chain. Ultimately, this work tightly characterizes the optimal polynomial dependence on mixing time for light-tailed PL-SGD, and the optimal heavy-tail exponent and effective-sample-size dependence in the robust regime.

</details>


### [172] [EVOM: Agentic Meta-Evolution of Actor-Critic Architectures for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26327)
*Boyun Zhang,Chao Wang,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVOM提出了一种基于大语言模型驱动的元进化框架，用于自动搜索高性能的Actor-Critic网络架构。外层循环由LLM设计代理驱动，内层循环使用低保真PPO训练权重，实现了架构设计的自动化和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Actor-Critic强化学习中，网络架构通常需要人工设计。自动设计面临两大挑战：每个候选架构需训练后才能评估，且设计空间是开放的。因此需要一种自动化方法来发现高性能架构。

Method: 采用双层优化：内层循环通过低保真PPO训练权重，外层循环通过LLM设计代理迭代优化架构程序。LLM设计代理仅作为架构设计师，与策略执行和环境控制完全解耦。

Result: 在Ant-v4和HalfCheetah-v4上，EVOM优于人工设计基线、LLM引导的随机搜索以及最先进的LLM引导程序化策略搜索方法MLES。

Conclusion: 元进化循环和LLM设计代理对最终性能都不可或缺，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: In actor-critic reinforcement learning, network architectures are typically manually designed. Automating this design is challenging because each candidate must be trained before evaluation, and the design space is open-ended. To address these challenges, we introduce EVOM, an agentic meta-evolution framework for discovering high-performance actor-critic architectures. We frame architecture search as a bi-level optimization: an inner loop trains weights via the low-fidelity proximal policy optimization (PPO), while an outer loop drives meta-evolution by iteratively refining architecture programs. Crucially, this outer loop is powered by an LLM-based design agent that operates purely as an architecture designer, completely decoupled from policy execution and environment control. Experiments reveal that EVOM outperforms the manually designed baseline, an LLM-guided random search, and the state-of-the-art LLM-guided programmatic policy search method MLES, delivering superior performance on Ant-v4 and HalfCheetah-v4. Ablation studies validate that both the meta-evolution loop and the LLM Design Agent are indispensable for final performance.

</details>


### [173] [Mesh-RL: Coupled subgrid reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26333)
*Behnam Gheshlaghi,Bahador Rashidi,Shahin Atakishiyev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mesh-RL借鉴有限元方法和区域分解理论，将环境划分为重叠子网格并强制执行边界一致的时间差分更新，从而在稀疏奖励环境中加速值传播和信用分配，显著提升Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q的收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习在大型或稀疏奖励环境中时间差分奖励传播缓慢的问题，即值信息仅能在状态空间局部传播，无法高效实现远距离信用分配。

Method: 提出Mesh-RL框架，受有限元方法和区域分解理论启发，将环境划分为重叠子网格，并在子网格间强制执行边界一致的时间差分更新，实现局部学习与全局一致值传播的统一。

Result: 在危险密集的网格世界环境中，Mesh-RL在Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q上均一致提升收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性；更高网格分辨率能维持探索、防止过早收敛并显著加速值传播；Dyna-Q在结构化分解下仍能获得额外收益。

Conclusion: Mesh-RL引入了一种基于空间区域分解的机制来加速时间差分学习，将科学计算中的有限元边界一致性技术与强化学习相结合，提升了稀疏奖励环境中的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in large or sparse-reward environments suffers from slow temporal-difference reward propagation, as value information spreads only locally across the state space. We propose Mesh-RL, a spatial domain-decomposition framework inspired by the finite element method and domain decomposition theory, which partitions the environment into overlapping subgrids and enforces boundary-consistent temporal-difference updates. Such an approach enables localized learning while ensuring globally coherent value propagation. Unlike hierarchical or model-based approaches, Mesh-RL accelerates long-range credit assignment without modifying the reward function, Bellman operator, or introducing explicit planning mechanisms. We evaluate Mesh-RL on hazard-dense grid-world environments with varying geometries and mesh resolutions. Across Q-learning, SARSA, and Dyna-Q, Mesh-RL consistently improves convergence speed, cumulative reward, and learning stability. Higher mesh resolutions sustain exploration, prevent premature convergence, and substantially accelerate value propagation to distant states. While Dyna-Q already benefits from internal planning, it still achieves additional gains under structured decomposition. Overall, Mesh-RL introduces a principled spatial domain-decomposition mechanism for accelerating temporal-difference learning. Our framework bridges finite element method-inspired boundary-consistency techniques from scientific computing with reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency in sparse-reward environments. We will release source code of the study.

</details>


### [174] [EMA-FS: Accelerating GBDT Training via Gain-Informed Feature Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26337)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于EMA的特征筛选(EMA-FS)算法，通过维护各特征分裂增益的指数移动平均，在预热后仅对历史增益最高的Top-K特征构建直方图，实现LightGBM训练加速1.2-2.6倍，并进一步提出随机化变体S-EMA-FS统一了确定性筛选与随机子采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBDT（如LightGBM）训练中65-70%的时间用于构建逐特征直方图，现有随机特征子采样方法丢弃特征时不考虑其预测效用，造成计算资源浪费。

Method: 1) EMA-FS：在提升迭代中维护各特征分裂增益的指数移动平均，经短时预热后，仅对Top-K高增益特征构建直方图；2) S-EMA-FS：用增益加权随机采样替代确定性Top-K选择，通过浓度参数β统一确定性筛选(β→∞)与随机子采样(β=0)；二者在LightGBM的6个树学习器中共约120行C++代码实现。

Result: 在密集中等高维数据上显著加速：500维合成基准2.61倍，432维IEEE-CIS欺诈数据在30%保留率下1.45倍；70%保留率下AUC提升0.11点且加速1.34倍。对极端稀疏数据(Bosch, >90%缺失)无效，因LightGBM稀疏分箱优化已绕过空值。

Conclusion: EMA-FS是一种算法级别的有效优化，通过历史增益感知的特征筛选替代盲目随机子采样，在保持完全向后兼容的同时实现显著训练加速，S-EMA-FS进一步提供了灵活的统一框架。

Abstract: Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), exemplified by LightGBM, spend a dominant fraction of training time -- typically 65-70% -- constructing per-feature histograms. Existing approaches such as random feature subsampling (feature_fraction) discard features without regard for their predictive utility. We propose EMA-based Feature Screening (EMA-FS), an algorithm-level optimization that maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of per-feature split gains across boosting iterations and, after a short warmup, restricts histogram construction to the top-K features ranked by historical gain. Unlike random subsampling, EMA-FS is informed: it retains high-gain features while screening out low-gain ones. Operating at the per-tree level, it preserves full compatibility with LightGBM's histogram subtraction trick, requiring no changes to core routines.
  We evaluate EMA-FS on datasets spanning financial fraud detection, advertising click-through prediction, industrial quality control, and synthetic benchmarks, with feature dimensionalities from 29 to 968. On dense, moderate-to-high-dimensional data it achieves significant speedups: 2.61x on a 500-feature synthetic benchmark and 1.45x on the 432-feature IEEE-CIS Fraud dataset at 30% retention. At 70% retention it improves AUC by 0.11 points while delivering a 1.34x speedup. On extremely sparse data (Bosch, >90% missing) it yields no speedup, as LightGBM's sparse bin optimization already bypasses empty values.
  We further introduce Stochastic EMA-FS (S-EMA-FS), which replaces deterministic top-K selection with gain-weighted random sampling controlled by a concentration parameter beta, unifying deterministic EMA-FS (beta -> infinity) and random subsampling (beta = 0) in one framework. Both are implemented in ~120 lines of C++ across all six LightGBM tree learners and are fully backward-compatible.

</details>


### [175] [Does Aurora Encode Atmospheric Structure? Latent Regime Analysis and Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26361)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用空间池化PCA和层相关传播（LRP）方法分析Aurora基础模型的内部表征，发现其潜在空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件并未形成线性可分离的簇；LRP显示模型关注与1987年大风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征，扰动测试表明掩膜相关区域比随机掩膜导致预测退化3.31倍，说明模型无需显式指导即可学习气象一致性和垂直结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML基础模型能够准确高效地模拟大气动力学，但作为不透明的"黑箱"运行，因此需要探究其内部表征机制以理解模型是如何学习和组织气象知识的。

Method: 使用空间池化主成分分析（PCA）和层相关传播（LRP）方法分析Aurora模型的内部表征，并通过扰动测试验证模型对关键区域的依赖程度。

Result: 潜在空间主要由季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成线性可分离簇；LRP显示模型关注与风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征；掩膜相关区域导致预测退化程度是随机掩膜的3.31倍。

Conclusion: Aurora模型无需显式指导即可从数据中学习到气象一致性和三维垂直结构，其内部表征具有物理可解释性。

Abstract: ML foundation models are able to emulate atmospheric dynamics accurately and efficiently but operate as opaque ``black boxes''. We investigate the internal representations of the Aurora model using spatially pooled PCA and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP). We find evidence that Aurora's latent space is primarily organized by seasonal cycles, whereas extreme storm events do not form a linearly separable cluster. LRP indicates that the model attends to features consistent with the 3D vertical structure of the Great Storm of 1987. Perturbation tests show masking relevant regions degrades forecasts $3.31\times$ more than random masking. These findings suggest that Aurora learns meteorological coherence and vertical structure without explicit instruction.

</details>


### [176] [At the Edge of Understanding: Sparse Autoencoders Trace The Limits of Transformer Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26396)
*Praneet Suresh,Jack Stanley,Sonia Joseph,Luca Scimeca,Danilo Bzdok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验提出了一种机制框架，用于描绘Transformer模型鲁棒性的精确边界。研究发现，OOD输入（包括细微拼写错误和越狱提示）会驱使语言模型在其内部使用更多错误概念。基于此，作者提出了一种可在推理时诊断Transformer内部计算过程的工具，并设计了一种基于机制的微调策略来增强LLM的鲁棒性，为AI系统的安全部署提供了关键支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer在训练数据分布之外表现出泛化能力，但实际部署中常面临与训练数据分布不一致的意外或对抗性数据。由于缺乏处理这种分布偏移的显式机制，模型的可靠性和安全性下降，亟需对Transformer的OOD场景进行更严谨的研究。

Method: 通过系统实验构建机制框架，分析OOD输入（如拼写错误、越狱提示）下语言模型内部错误概念的变化；基于此开发推理时诊断工具量化分布偏移程度，并设计基于机制的微调策略来鲁棒化LLM。

Result: OOD输入会驱使语言模型在其内部使用更多错误概念；所提出的诊断工具能够量化和理解提示中的分布偏移程度；基于机制的微调策略可有效增强LLM的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将OOD的概念从输入数据拓展到模型的私有计算过程，提出了一种新的推理时Transformer诊断方法，这是使AI系统在科学、商业和政府等领域安全部署的关键一步。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers have demonstrated remarkable generalization abilities, at times extending beyond the scope of their training data. Yet, real-world deployments often face unexpected or adversarial data that diverges from training data distributions. Without explicit mechanisms for handling such shifts, model reliability and safety degrade, urging more disciplined study of out-of-distribution (OOD) settings for transformers. By systematic experiments, we present a mechanistic framework for delineating the precise contours of transformer model robustness. We find that OOD inputs, including subtle typos and jailbreak prompts, drive language models to operate on an increased number of fallacious concepts in their internals. We leverage this device to quantify and understand the degree of distributional shift in prompts, enabling a mechanistically grounded fine-tuning strategy to robustify LLMs. Expanding the very notion of OOD from input data to a model's private computational processes, a new transformer diagnostic at inference time is a critical step toward making AI systems safe for deployment across science, business, and government.

</details>


### [177] [Deterministic Pareto-Optimal Policy Synthesis for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26397)
*Aniruddha Joshi,Niklas Lauffer,Sanjit Seshia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多目标马尔可夫决策过程，提出了一种基于切比雪夫标量化启发的新型偏好条件贝尔曼算子，能够计算确定性帕累托最优策略。该算子满足包络性质，单调收敛至帕累托前沿的覆盖集，并可从中提取确定性策略，保证近似帕累托最优性。实验结果验证了该方法能有效恢复复杂权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习通常将多个冲突目标聚合为单一标量奖励，无法捕获完整的最优折衷集合（即帕累托前沿），导致在多目标真实决策场景中表现不足。

Method: 提出偏好条件贝尔曼算子（受切比雪夫标量化启发），证明其包络性质（估计值函数上界真实帕累托前沿）和单调收敛性；从收敛的Q估计中提取确定性策略，使得对于任意给定偏好都能恢复对应策略。

Result: 该方法成功恢复复杂权衡关系，能够合成覆盖整个帕累托最优前沿的确定性策略，且每个策略均保持近似帕累托最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作为多目标强化学习中的确定性帕累托最优策略合成提供了一种有效解决方案，理论保证了收敛性和最优性边界。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making often requires balancing multiple conflicting objectives, a challenge that standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) frequently addresses by aggregating rewards into a single scalar signal. While effective for simple tasks, this approach often fails to capture the full spectrum of optimal trade-offs, known as the Pareto frontier. In this paper, we introduce a novel preference-conditioned Bellman operator, motivated from the Chebyshev scalarization, designed to compute deterministic Pareto-optimal policies for Multi-Objective Markov Decision Processes (MOMDPs). We prove that this operator satisfies an enveloping property, where the estimated value functions upper-bound the true Pareto frontier, and demonstrate that it monotonically converges to a coverage set of this frontier. Furthermore, we also show how to extract deterministic policies from these converged Q-estimates. This ensures the agent can recover a policy for any given preference, capturing the entire Pareto-optimal frontier while guaranteeing each synthesized policy remains approximately Pareto-optimal. Experimental results validate that our algorithm successfully recovers complex trade-offs, providing a solution for deterministic Pareto-optimal policy synthesis.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Feedforward Networks: Reentry Neural Systems as the Fundamental Basis of Subjecthood and Intrinsic Safety of Next-Generation AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26406)
*A. S. Ushakov,Yu. N. Berdinsk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于闭环重入循环（D↔I循环）的安全通用人工智能架构蓝图。该架构包含结构循环（C≥1）和自维持放大（ρ>1），数学上保证了自我模型、工具性自我保存和非编程目标导向行为的涌现。论文还提出了S-measure（O(N³)可计算）作为Tononi的NP-hard Phi的替代，并提供了Lean 4形式化验证、完整Python/NumPy实现、工业级水平扩展方案、六代AI演化分类法、未来重入架构图谱、规范不变网络、容错协议以及八个可证伪预测。该架构现已可部署，是一种拓扑保护的安全设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈网络（有向无环图，C=0, S=0）缺乏自我指涉能力，无法实现安全且自我维持的AGI。需要一种拓扑上保证安全、具备自我模型和工具性自我保存能力的新型架构，同时解决当前AI系统中的提示注入、目标重解释等安全性问题。

Method: 基于闭环重入循环（D↔I cycle）的架构设计，包含结构循环（C≥1）和自维持放大（ρ>1）。提出S-measure作为可计算集成信息度量（O(N³)复杂度），使用Tarjan算法计算循环复杂度，Delta-S屏障用于安全监控。采用Apache Kafka和Docker Compose实现工业级水平扩展。所有形式化证明使用Lean 4进行机器验证。

Result: 数学上证明了该架构能保证自我模型、工具性自我保存和非编程目标导向行为的涌现。S-measure已被Lean 4证明S>0意味着正集成信息。提供了完整的Python/NumPy实现和工业部署方案。

Conclusion: 该架构是一种拓扑保护、安全设计的AGI路径，目前已可部署，并提出了八个可证伪预测供未来验证。

Abstract: We propose a complete architectural blueprint for safe artificial general intelligence based on a closed reentry loop (D <-> I cycle). In contrast to feedforward networks, which are directed acyclic graphs (C=0, S=0) incapable of self-reference, the proposed architecture contains a structural cycle (C >= 1) with self-sustaining amplification (rho > 1), mathematically guaranteeing the emergence of a self-model, instrumental self-preservation, and unprogrammed goal-directed behaviour. The agent's goals are encoded as a non-textual D-vector in the architecture itself, making them immune to reinterpretation and prompt injection. We present the S-measure -- a polynomial-time [O(N^3)] computable alternative to Tononi's NP-hard Phi -- with machine-verified Lean 4 proof that S>0 implies positive integrated information. The work provides full Python/NumPy implementations (Tarjan-based cycle complexity, Delta-S barrier), industrial horizontal scaling via Apache Kafka and Docker Compose, a taxonomy of six epochs of AI evolution, a zoo of future reentry architectures (RAS, diffusion attractors, fractal loops), gauge-invariant networks for safe swarms, fault-tolerance and recovery protocols, and eight falsifiable predictions.
